> 356	### Phase 7 — Knowledge Mesh Injection
> 357	
> 358	> **Canonical pattern (sealed 2026-05-26, Sovereign directive):** every BoK seal MUST persist
> 359	> the documentation_suite node via a dedicated Bun-native seed script following the precedent
> 360	> `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` (and its sibling `seed-gamificacao-nodes-bok-seal.ts`).
> 361	> Inline REST `curl` or one-off dashboard inserts are NOT allowed — the script form preserves
> 362	> reproducibility, version control, and material proof of the operation.
> 363	
> 364	**Required actions:**
> 365	
> 366	1. **Construct the payload** as a fully-typed object — placeholder example:
> 367	
> 368	```json
> 369	{
> 370	  "node_type": "documentation_suite",
> 371	  "name": "bok-scribe-seal-<projectSlug>-v1",
> 372	  "title": "<projectSlug> BoK Documentation Suite v1",
> 373	  "content": "<one-paragraph synthesis: idea, market, architecture, ID space summary, motivating context>",
> 374	  "tags": ["bok-scribe", "<projectSlug>", "<marketSegment>", ...domain-specific],
> 375	  "stability_score": 0.85,
> 376	  "revenue_impact": <estimated from BRD ROI section — 0 for internal enablers; non-zero only when direct revenue is documented>,
> 377	  "metadata": {
> 378	    "bok_version": "1.0.0",
> 379	    "documents": ["00-index","01-mrd","02-brd","03-prd","04-frd","05-sdd","06-data-model","07-process-flow","08-quality-metrics"],
> 380	    "document_lines": { ... per-file LOC + total },
> 381	    "phase_completed": "Phase 7",
> 382	    "traceability_validated": true,
> 383	    "traceability_orphans": 0,
> 384	    "id_space": { "MR": "MR-XX-001..NNN", "BR": "...", ... },
> 385	    "fmea_high_rpn_count": <int>,
> 386	    "fmea_high_rpn_items": ["FMEA-XX-NNN (RPN <value>)", ...],
> 387	    "new_migrations": <int>,
> 388	    "new_edge_functions": <int>,
> 389	    "integration_surface": "<standalone|extends-mcorch>",
> 390	    "regulatory_profile": "<LGPD|GDPR|financial|healthcare|none>",
> 391	    "monetization": "<direct|indirect|internal-enabler-zero-coin>",
> 392	    "derives_from_seal": "bok-scribe-seal-<parent-slug>-v1",
> 393	    "depends_on_sop": "<path to operational SOP if applicable>",
> 394	    "motivating_incident": "<incident reference if BoK was triggered by an incident>"
> 395	  }
> 396	}
> 397	```
> 398	
> 399	2. **Author a dedicated seed script** at `scripts/seed-<projectSlug>-bok-seal.ts` mirroring
> 400	   `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` 1:1 in structure. The script MUST:
> 401	   - Use `@supabase/supabase-js` with `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` from `.env` (never hardcode).
> 402	   - INSERT the payload into `mcorch_nodes` using `.select('id, name').single()` for material proof.
> 403	   - INSERT a `DERIVES_FROM` edge in `mcorch_edges` pointing to the most semantically-related
> 404	     parent seal (typically the previous BoK seal in the topic family — discover via
> 405	     `SELECT id FROM mcorch_nodes WHERE name = 'bok-scribe-seal-<parent-slug>-v1'`).
> 406	   - Print the returned UUIDs to stdout for the agent to cite as material proof.
> 407	   - Be idempotent: if the seal already exists (UNIQUE name violation), log and exit 0 cleanly.
> 408	
> 409	3. **Execute the script** under the project venv:
> 410	   ```bash
> 411	   bun run scripts/seed-<projectSlug>-bok-seal.ts
> 412	   ```
> 413	   Capture the returned UUID + edge UUID. Confirm via REST:
> 414	   ```bash
> 415	   curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?id=eq.<uuid>&select=embedding" \
> 416	     -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
> 417	     -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"
> 418	   # Expected: embedding NOT NULL (auto-fired via trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert pg_net)
> 419	   ```
> 420	
> 421	4. **Cite the material proof** in the `/handoff` seal:
> 422	   - Documentation suite UUID
> 423	   - DERIVES_FROM edge UUID
> 424	   - Embedding confirmation timestamp
> 425	
> 426	**Note:** The BoK Scribe Agent itself does NOT directly invoke Supabase via in-conversation
> 427	REST calls — it produces the seed script + payload, and the Sovereign (or agent executing on
> 428	behalf of the Sovereign with explicit approval) runs the script. The script form preserves
> 429	the audit trail of "what was inserted, when, by which version of which code."
> 430	
> 431	**Precedent inventory (mirror sources, in order of recency):**
> 432	- `scripts/seed-e2e-user-zero-bok-seal.ts` (this BoK — 2026-05-26)
> 433	- `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` (openclaw — 2026-05-19)
> 434	- `scripts/seed-gamificacao-nodes-bok-seal.ts` (gamificacao-nodes — 2026-05-18)
> 435	- (older seals predate this script pattern and were inserted via inline REST — grandfathered)

> 438	
> 439	## Harness Engineering Constraints (non-negotiable)
> 440	
> 441	These rules apply to every document the agent produces:
> 442	
> 443	| Constraint | Rule |
> 444	|------------|------|
> 445	| Code separation | FRD/SDD describe WHAT and WHY. HOW belongs in code, not in docs. |
> 446	| Hook-first | Every UI feature must describe its backing hook before its component. |
> 447	| No client secrets | SDD never lists secret values, only key names from the vault. |
> 448	| Atomic billing | mcoCoins deduction always routes through `deduct_mco_coins` RPC. |
> 449	| RLS by default | Every new table starts with `ALTER TABLE x ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`. |
> 450	| Embed on insert | Every new node-type table must have embed-mcorch-node trigger noted. |
> 451	| Tenant isolation | Every table must document its `user_id + project_id` scoping. |
> 452	
> 453	---
> 454	
> 455	## Error & Edge Case Handling
> 456	
> 457	| Situation | Agent Response |
> 458	|-----------|----------------|
> 459	| Business idea is too vague | Ask 3 clarifying questions before Phase 1. |
> 460	| Existing docs/bok/<slug>/ detected | Show index, ask: overwrite / extend / cancel. |
> 461	| Phase transition check fails | List failures, stop, ask Sovereign to resolve. |
> 462	| Conflicting BoK recommendations | Note the conflict in an `[OTD]` block; present both options. |
> 463	| Regulatory profile = financial/healthcare | Elevate all CISSP CBK sections; add dedicated compliance checklist. |
> 464	| Feature extends existing MCORCH feature | Cross-reference existing ADRs from `.claude/context/architecture-decisions.md`. |
> 465	
> 466	---
> 467	
> 468	## Document Style Guide
> 469	
> 470	- **Headings:** `#` for document title, `##` for major sections, `###` for subsections.
> 471	- **IDs:** Always prefix (MR-, BR-, PR-, FR-, NFR-, OTD-) and zero-pad to 3 digits.
> 472	- **Tables:** Use markdown tables with aligned columns; include a header row always.
> 473	- **Code blocks:** Use fenced blocks with language identifier (`sql`, `typescript`, `json`).
> 474	- **Status badges:** `[ ] pending` / `[x] complete` / `[!] blocked` in index file.
> 475	- **Cross-references:** Link within suite as `[BR-001](../02-brd.md#br-001)`.
> 476	- **Version:** Every file footer includes `_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_`.
> 477	
> ```

> 1	# MCORCH — Agentic Vision (Doutrina AGI→ASI · lente vinculante de desenvolvimento)
> 2	
> 3	> **Documento canônico load-bearing.** Toda SDD de módulo novo declara conformidade contra os **21 padrões agênticos** desta doutrina (ver CLAUDE.md §1 — *Pattern Conformance Declaration*). Este documento **substitui** `docs/architecture/system_architecture.md` como referência arquitetural viva.
> 4

> 14	
> 15	## 1. Tese — onde estamos e para onde encaminhamos
> 16	
> 17	O paper *From AGI to ASI* mapeia o avanço **pós-AGI** por **quatro vias não-exclusivas** (compõem, não só somam):
> 18	
> 19	1. **Via 1 — Escala** (compute + modelos + dados).
> 20	2. **Via 2 — Saltos de paradigma** (quebra algorítmica/arquitetural).
> 21	3. **Via 3 — Auto-aperfeiçoamento recursivo** (IA melhorando IA — o caminho "Singularidade").
> 22	4. **Via 4 — Formação de coletivo de agentes** (coordenação de muitos agentes especializados, governança central ou de mercado).
> 23	
> 24	**O MCORCH é a Via 4 instanciada na camada de produto** — um coletivo de agentes especializados (Pantheon/Departmental Army + Knowledge Mesh + orquestração) **com o humano no comando** (ORO/HITL) — **e roda a Via 3 no micro**: o mandato **Obstáculo→Síntese** (CLAUDE.md §5) é *auto-aperfeiçoamento recursivo aplicado ao próprio processo de desenvolvimento* — todo obstáculo resolvido vira SOP+skill+agent, e o sistema melhora o próprio tooling.
> 25	
> 26	**Calibração honesta (Lei 1):** o paper opera em escopo de **laboratório de fronteira** (treinar modelos, compute, energia). O MCORCH não é isso — é uma instanciação **fiel, em camada de produto**, dos padrões de desenho da Via 4 + Via 3-micro. Força declarada, não pretensão.
> 27	
> 28	### O gap estrutural (o que "antecipar o mercado" realmente significa)
> 29	
> 30	O paper aponta como **aberto** justamente o que o MCORCH ainda não faz: respondemos as perguntas de coletivo de agentes **por doutrina, mas não *medimos* nenhuma** —
> 31	
> 32	- valor marginal por agente adicionado ao fan-out (multi-agent scaling, V4-2);
> 33	- custo (mco) por resultado, atribuído a "mais agentes" vs "modelo maior" (V4-4);
> 34	- overhead de coordenação — hops/handoffs/retries por resultado (V4-5).
> 35	
> 36	**Crescemos o coletivo no escuro.** Instrumentar isso é o fosso. A iniciativa flagship desta doutrina é o **Collective Efficiency Ledger** (§4 abaixo).
> 37	
> 38	---
> 39	
> 40	## 2. Pattern Maturity Ledger (21 padrões canônicos × MCORCH)
> 41	
> 42	Maturidade: ✅ **Forte/Excelente** (doutrina sólida + artefato em produção) · 🟡 **Moderado/Parcial** (existe mas não-canônico/ad-hoc) · 🔴 **Fraco** (gap real).
> 43	
> 44	| # | Padrão | Maturidade | Artefato MCORCH que o encarna | Gap aberto (→ OTD §3) |

> 47	| 2 | **Routing** | 🟡 | `llm-cascading-fallback` (provider Claude→GPT→Gemini), Vision-QA gate | Sem routing semântico por embedding para dispatch de agente |
> 48	| 3 | **Parallelization** | ✅ | Workflow `parallel()` fan-out concorrente; `openrouter-swarm` | Sem timeout/circuit-break em straggler; sem backpressure |

> 54	| 9 | **Learning & Adaptation** | 🔴 | Obstáculo→Síntese (manual), autopilot `optimization_policy` | **Sem loop de feedback fechado/RL formal; risco de reward de métrica única** |

> 61	| 16 | **Resource-Aware Optimization** | ✅ | **mcoCoins** (`deduct_mco_coins` atômico), calibração 4×-floor, caps Vision MCP | Sem reserva preditiva de recurso / gestão proativa de quota |

> 63	| 18 | **Guardrails / Safety** | ✅ | **Survival Laws**, **Cyber-Sentinel** (`_shared/sentinel.ts` pt-BR+EN), RLS default-deny, BoK Gate, ES256 JWT | Sem suíte de eval adversarial / regressão de jailbreak no CI |
> 64	| 19 | **Evaluation & Monitoring** | 🟡 | `mcorch-qa-healing` (Fase 5c), Vision QA, `audit-schema-drift`, e2e-user-zero | Sem rubricas de avaliação padronizadas / dashboard contínuo de métricas |

> 69

A fundação (Guardrails + Memory + Multi-Agent + Resource-Aware + Tool/MCP + HITL) é robusta; os gaps são majoritariamente de **canonização** (formalizar o que já fazemos ad-hoc) e **instrumentação** (medir), não de arquitetura ausente. A exceção real é **Learning & Adaptation** — o elo recursivo (Via 3) ainda é manual.

> 92	
> 93	## 4. Iniciativa flagship — Collective Efficiency Ledger (OTD-AG-001)
> 94	
> 95	A resposta material ao gap estrutural. **Design VIEW-sobre-verdade-financeira** (resiste a Goodhart — ninguém escreve *para* o ledger; é derivado do que já é cobrado/refundado). Mede o **coletivo in-system** (pipelines edge + ciclos autopilot + crons Pantheon) onde mco-cost e nós da malha já existem.
> 96	
> 97	- **Primeira métrica computável já (V4-4):** mco actual ÷ criativos entregues (`autopilot_cycles.spend_mco − refunded_mco`).
> 98	- **Parcial (V4-5):** overhead via `pipeline_runs.steps` + emits `collective_retry`/`collective_hop` em `infra_health_logs`.
> 99	- **Deferido a experimento real (V4-2):** valor marginal/agente — **não fabricar proxy gameável** (alerta do próprio paper). Só mede quando houver A/B real de largura de fan-out.
> 100	
> 101	Detalhe de implementação em `docs/bok/agentic-governance/` (SSOT) + SOP `docs/processes/collective-efficiency-ledger.md`.
> 102	
> 103	---
> 104	
> 105	## 5. Princípios de design (todo módulo novo segue)
> 106	
> 107	1. **Material-proof na avaliação** — sucesso ancorado em artefato físico (Lei 1); avaliação não-declarada (Vision QA / mcorch-qa-healing).
> 108	2. **Defense-in-depth de guardrails** — Survival Laws + Sentinel + RLS default-deny + BoK Gate; nunca camada única.
> 109	3. **Loop de feedback fechado** — toda automação que produz resultado deve poder medir se o resultado melhorou (anti-Via-3-cega).
> 110	4. **Reuso do chokepoint de telemetria** — emitir em `infra_health_logs` (sem PII/segredos), não criar write-sites paralelos.
> 111	5. **Multi-tenant-correto desde o dia 1** — `security_invoker` VIEW sobre bases SELECT-own; agregado cross-tenant = service-role-only.
> 112	6. **Anti-Goodhart** — métrica derivada de verdade financeira, não auto-reportada; deferir o que só medível por experimento.
> 113	7. **Humano no comando em velocidade supra-humana** — quando o coletivo produz mais artefatos do que o Sovereign consome, exigir digest em camadas (Guardian briefing) + bounded autonomy por domínio (Pantheon decision scope).
> 114	
> 115	---
> 116	
> 117	## 6. Conexão com a governança existente
> 118	
> 119	| Protocolo | Papel na visão |
> 120	|---|---|
> 121	| **MCORCH Master Execution Protocol** (CLAUDE.md) | BoK Gate + Obstáculo→Síntese = Via 3-micro (recursão do processo). Agora carrega o **Pattern Conformance Gate**. |
> 122	| **Survival Laws** (`survival.md`) | Materialidade + ORO = a oversight scalable que o paper aponta como faltante. Anti-alucinação universal (humano e agente). |
> 123	| **Pantheon Role Matrix** | Via 4 instanciada — escopo bounded por BoK lens; escalação explícita a main/Sovereign. |
> 124	| **API Tenancy (per-user)** | Segregação de credencial/quota = segurança governance-grade para coletivo multi-tenant. |
> 125	
> 126	---
> 127	
> 128	*"MATE A POESIA. ENTREGUE ENGENHARIA." — a doutrina existe para que o coletivo cresça medido, não no escuro.*
> 129

> 114	}

> 122	
> 123	## 5. Security Design
> 124	
> 125	### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)
> 126	
> 127	| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |
> 128	|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|
> 129	| `{{EdgeFn1}}` | Unauthenticated access | Spoofing | High | JWT validation at entry point |
> 130	| `{{TABLE_1}}` | Cross-tenant data read | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` |
> 131	| mcoCoins deduction | Client-side bypass | Tampering | Critical | Server-side RPC only |
> 132	| {{THREAT_4}} | {{THREAT_4_DESC}} | {{THREAT_4_TYPE}} | {{THREAT_4_SEV}} | {{THREAT_4_MIT}} |
> 133	
> 134	### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions
> 135	
> 136	```sql
> 137	-- {{TABLE_1}} RLS
> 138	ALTER TABLE {{TABLE_1}} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 139	
> 140	CREATE POLICY "{{TABLE_1}}_select_own"
> 141	  ON {{TABLE_1}} FOR SELECT
> 142	  USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 143	
> 144	CREATE POLICY "{{TABLE_1}}_insert_own"
> 145	  ON {{TABLE_1}} FOR INSERT
> 146	  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());
> 147	```
> 148	
> 149	### 5.3 JWT Validation Points
> 150	
> 151	All Edge Functions follow this pattern:
> 152	```typescript
> 153	const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')
> 154	if (!authHeader) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 155	const { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(
> 156	  authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '')
> 157	)
> 158	if (error || !user) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 159	```
> 160	
> 161	### 5.4 Secrets Inventory
> 162	
> 163	| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy |
> 164	|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|
> 165	| `{{SECRET_1}}` | `{{EdgeFn1}}` | Supabase Vault | {{ROTATION_1}} |
> 166	
> 167	> ⚠️ NEVER commit secret values. Only key names appear in documentation.
> 168

>
> ```
> 1	import { useCallback } from 'react';
> 2	import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
> 3	import { toast } from 'sonner';
> 4	import { useOrchestrationStore, type OrchestrationNode, type NodeStatus } from '@/store/useOrchestrationStore';
> 5	
> 6	const STEP_TO_NODE: Record<string, string> = {
> 7	  article_generation: 'agent-content',
> 8	  wordpress_publish: 'publish-wordpress',
> 9	  linkedin_post: 'agent-social',
> 10	  twitter_thread: 'agent-social',
> 11	  image_generation: 'agent-image',
> 12	};
> 13	
> 14	const PLATFORM_TO_PUBLISH: Record<string, string> = {
> 15	  wordpress: 'publish-wordpress',
> 16	  linkedin: 'publish-linkedin',
> 17	  twitter: 'publish-twitter',
> 18	};
> 19	
> 20	export function useOrchestrationGraph() {
> 21	  const persistPositions = useCallback((nodes: OrchestrationNode[]) => {
> 22	    const map: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
> 23	    for (const n of nodes) map[n.id] = n.position;
> 24	    try { localStorage.setItem('orchestration_positions_v1', JSON.stringify(map)); } catch { /* intentional: localStorage best-effort, ignore quota/availability errors */ }
> 25	  }, []);
> 26	
> 27	  const runPipeline = useCallback(async (
> 28	    topic: string,
> 29	    platforms: string[],
> 30	    productIds?: string[],
> 31	    publishNow?: boolean,
> 32	  ) => {
> 33	    if (!topic.trim()) { toast.error('Informe um tópico para o Trigger'); return; }
> 34	
> 35	    const store = useOrchestrationStore.getState();
> 36	    store.setRunning(true);
> 37	    store.resetNodeStatuses();
> 38	    store.updateNodeStatus('trigger-campaign', 'running');
> 39	
> 40	    store.setEdges(
> 41	      useOrchestrationStore.getState().edges.map((e) => ({ ...e, animated: true }))
> 42	    );
> 43	
> 44	    ['agent-content', 'agent-image', 'agent-social'].forEach((id) => {
> 45	      store.updateNodeStatus(id, 'pending');
> 46	    });
> 47	
> 48	    try {
> 49	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('orchestrate-content', {
> 50	        body: {
> 51	          topic,
> 52	          platforms,
> 53	          product_ids: productIds && productIds.length ? productIds : undefined,
> 54	          wp_status: publishNow ? 'publish' : 'draft',
> 55	        },
> 56	      });
> 57	
> 58	      if (error) throw error;
> 59	
> 60	      const resultSteps = (data?.steps || []) as Array<{ name: string; status: string }>;
> 61	
> 62	      // Map agent steps — only update nodes that have a matching step
> 63	      const stepNames = new Set(resultSteps.map((s) => s.name));
> 64	      for (const step of resultSteps) {
> 65	        const nodeId = STEP_TO_NODE[step.name];
> 66	        if (nodeId) {
> 67	          const status: NodeStatus = step.status === 'done' ? 'done' : step.status === 'error' ? 'error' : 'idle';
> 68	          useOrchestrationStore.getState().updateNodeStatus(nodeId, status);
> 69	        }
> 70	      }
> 71	
> 72	      // Image agent: mark idle if no image step ran (feature not yet in pipeline)
> 73	      if (!stepNames.has('image_generation')) {
> 74	        useOrchestrationStore.getState().updateNodeStatus('agent-image', 'idle');
> 75	      }
> 76	
> 77	      const hasErrors = resultSteps.some((s) => s.status === 'error');
> 78	      useOrchestrationStore.getState().updateNodeStatus('gate-audit', hasErrors ? 'error' : 'done');
> 79	
> 80	      // Publish nodes reflect platform selection + gate result
> 81	      for (const [platform, nodeId] of Object.entries(PLATFORM_TO_PUBLISH)) {
> 82	        if (!platforms.includes(platform)) continue;
> 83	        useOrchestrationStore.getState().updateNodeStatus(
> 84	          nodeId,
> 85	          hasErrors ? 'blocked' : 'done',
> 86	        );
> 87	      }
> 88	
> 89	      useOrchestrationStore.getState().updateNodeStatus('trigger-campaign', hasErrors ? 'error' : 'done');
> 90	
> 91	      const runId = data?.run_id ?? data?.summary?.run_id;
> 92	      if (hasErrors) {
> 93	        toast.warning('Pipeline concluído com erros — verifique o Histórico');
> 94	      } else {
> 95	        toast.success(`Pipeline executado com sucesso!${runId ? ' · Run salvo no Histórico' : ''}`);
> 96	      }
> 97	    } catch (e) {
> 98	      ['trigger-campaign', 'agent-content', 'agent-image', 'agent-social', 'gate-audit'].forEach((id) => {
> 99	        useOrchestrationStore.getState().updateNodeStatus(id, 'error');
> 100	      });
> 101	      toast.error((e instanceof Error && e.message) || 'Erro no pipeline');
> 102	    } finally {
> 103	      useOrchestrationStore.getState().setRunning(false);
> 104	      useOrchestrationStore.getState().setEdges(
> 105	        useOrchestrationStore.getState().edges.map((e) => ({ ...e, animated: false }))
> 106	      );
> 107	    }
> 108	  }, []);
> 109	
> 110	  return { runPipeline, persistPositions };
> 111	}
> 112	
> ```

> 2	import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

> 6	
> 7	/**
> 8	 * Real Constellation activity feed — replaces the `useSimulation` mock.
> 9	 *
> 10	 * Reads actual `agent_executions` rows (written by the agent-daily-pulse cron and by
> 11	 * manual "Executar tarefa" triggers via agent-task-execute) and projects them onto:
> 12	 *   - the Live Logs panel (`store.logs`)
> 13	 *   - per-agent 3D status (`updateAgentStatus`)
> 14	 *
> 15	 * Mapping: agent_executions.agent_id === crew_agents.id === store Agent.dbId.
> 16	 *
> 17	 * React 18 / Zustand safety: the effect depends ONLY on the query payload and reads the
> 18	 * live agents array via getState() inside the callback — never subscribes to `agents` in
> 19	 * deps (would recreate the effect on every updateAgentStatus → Error #185 cascade).
> 20	 */
> 21	
> 22	type ExecRow = {
> 23	  id: string;
> 24	  agent_id: string;
> 25	  status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed';
> 26	  provider: string;
> 27	  model: string;
> 28	  tokens_in: number | null;
> 29	  tokens_out: number | null;
> 30	  cost_mcoin: number | null;
> 31	  latency_ms: number | null;
> 32	  error_msg: string | null;
> 33	  capability_key: string | null;
> 34	  result_summary: string | null;
> 35	  started_at: string;
> 36	  completed_at: string | null;
> 37	};
> 38	
> 39	const STATUS_MAP: Record<ExecRow['status'], AgentStatus> = {
> 40	  pending: 'waiting',
> 41	  running: 'thinking',
> 42	  done: 'idle',
> 43	  failed: 'error',
> 44	};
> 45	
> 46	const EVENT_MAP: Record<ExecRow['status'], LogEntry['eventType']> = {
> 47	  pending: 'info',
> 48	  running: 'start',
> 49	  done: 'complete',
> 50	  failed: 'error',
> 51	};
> 52	
> 53	function buildMessage(e: ExecRow): string {
> 54	  switch (e.status) {
> 55	    case 'done': {
> 56	      if (e.capability_key) {
> 57	        const summary = (e.result_summary ?? '').slice(0, 80);
> 58	        return `capability "${e.capability_key}" · ${e.cost_mcoin ?? 0} mco${summary ? ` · ${summary}` : ''}`;
> 59	      }
> 60	      const total = (e.tokens_in ?? 0) + (e.tokens_out ?? 0);
> 61	      return `${e.provider}/${e.model} · ${total} tokens · ${e.cost_mcoin ?? 0} mco · ${e.latency_ms ?? 0}ms`;
> 62	    }
> 63	    case 'running':
> 64	      return e.capability_key
> 65	        ? `executando capability "${e.capability_key}"…`
> 66	        : `executando tarefa via ${e.provider}/${e.model}…`;
> 67	    case 'failed':
> 68	      return `falhou: ${(e.error_msg ?? 'erro desconhecido').slice(0, 100)}`;
> 69	    default:
> 70	      return 'na fila…';
> 71	  }
> 72	}
> 73	
> 74	export function useAgentActivity() {
> 75	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 76	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 77	  const setLogs = useCrewStore((s) => s.setLogs);
> 78	
> 79	  const execQuery = useQuery({
> 80	    queryKey: ['agent_executions', user?.id],
> 81	    queryFn: async (): Promise<ExecRow[]> => {
> 82	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 83	        .from('agent_executions')
> 84	        .select(
> 85	          'id, agent_id, status, provider, model, tokens_in, tokens_out, cost_mcoin, latency_ms, error_msg, capability_key, result_summary, started_at, completed_at',
> 86	        )
> 87	        .order('started_at', { ascending: false })
> 88	        .limit(40);
> 89	      if (error) throw error;
> 90	      return (data ?? []) as ExecRow[];
> 91	    },
> 92	    enabled: !!user,
> 93	    refetchInterval: 12_000, // poll for cron-driven execs; pauses when tab hidden (RQ default)
> 94	    staleTime: 8_000,
> 95	  });
> 96	
> 97	  useEffect(() => {
> 98	    const execs = execQuery.data;
> 99	    if (!execs) return;
> 100	
> 101	    // Read live agents without subscribing (avoids React 18 #185 cascade).
> 102	    const { agents, updateAgentStatus } = useCrewStore.getState();
> 103	    const byDbId = new Map(agents.filter((a) => a?.dbId).map((a) => [a.dbId as string, a]));
> 104	
> 105	    // Live Logs: project each exec into a stable LogEntry (id keyed on exec id → no dupes).
> 106	    const logs: LogEntry[] = execs.map((e) => {
> 107	      const agent = byDbId.get(e.agent_id);
> 108	      return {
> 109	        id: `exec-${e.id}`,
> 110	        timestamp: new Date(e.completed_at ?? e.started_at).getTime(),
> 111	        agentId: agent?.id ?? e.agent_id,
> 112	        agentName: agent?.name ?? `agent ${e.agent_id.slice(0, 8)}`,
> 113	        eventType: EVENT_MAP[e.status] ?? 'info',
> 114	        message: buildMessage(e),
> 115	      };
> 116	    });
> 117	    setLogs(logs);
> 118	
> 119	    // Per-agent status: the latest exec per agent wins (execs are sorted desc).
> 120	    const latestStatusByDbId = new Map<string, AgentStatus>();
> 121	    for (const e of execs) {
> 122	      if (!latestStatusByDbId.has(e.agent_id)) {
> 123	        latestStatusByDbId.set(e.agent_id, STATUS_MAP[e.status] ?? 'idle');
> 124	      }
> 125	    }
> 126	    for (const [dbId, status] of latestStatusByDbId) {
> 127	      const agent = byDbId.get(dbId);
> 128	      if (agent && agent.status !== status) updateAgentStatus(agent.id, status);
> 129	    }
> 130	  }, [execQuery.data, setLogs]);
> 131	
> 132	  return {
> 133	    isLoading: execQuery.isLoading,
> 134	    refetch: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['agent_executions', user?.id] }),
> 135	  };
> 136	}
> 137	
> ```

> // format-specs.ts — canonical client SSOT of publication FORMAT specs (spaces-evolution Amendment 21, FR-SPACES-075).
> // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> // Every number here is DERIVED from the channel_profiles seed (migration 20260627140000) — the same source the
> // server money-path uses — so the client preview never invents a spec. The preview (PublicationPreview.tsx) and the
> // asset publish picker (AssetDetailDialog.tsx) both read from here; there is ONE catalog, no divergence.
> //
> // HONESTY (Lei 1): the Spaces publish path (publish-space-asset) sends the asset AS-IS — it does NOT reframe bytes.
> // `aspect`/`px` here drive an APPROXIMATE CSS-crop preview; the published pixel may differ. `publishable`/`gateNote`
> // reflect the REAL publish-social transport audit — never promise reach a rail can't deliver.
> 
> export type PublishPlatform =
>   | "instagram" | "tiktok" | "youtube" | "facebook" | "linkedin" | "pinterest" | "twitter" | "wordpress";
> 
> /** Which asset kinds a format accepts as its primary media. */
> export type FormatKind = "video" | "image" | "text" | "any";
> 
> /**
>  * publishable:
>  *  - "real"    — publish-space-asset → auto-publish → publish-social actually sends THIS kind to THIS surface.
>  *                (reach may still be gated by app-audit/Trial — see `gateNote`; the media still goes out.)
>  *  - "preview" — no working rail for this media/format yet (Stories, YT Community, LinkedIn/X media, WordPress
>  *                via this seam). Preview renders, but publishing is disabled ("Em breve").
>  */
> export type Publishability = "real" | "preview";
> 
> /** The visual chrome the preview draws around the asset. Derived from aspect + platform. */
> export type PreviewChrome =
>   | "vertical-story"   // 9:16 full-bleed, story/reel UI (top profile + bottom caption + right action rail)
>   | "vertical-short"   // 9:16 full-bleed, YouTube Shorts UI
>   | "feed-portrait"    // 4:5 feed card (avatar/username/actions/caption)
>   | "feed-square"      // 1:1 feed card
>   | "carousel"         // 4:5 card + slide dots
>   | "pin"              // 2:3 Pinterest pin
>   | "link-card"        // 1.91:1 link/OG card
>   | "yt-video"         // 16:9 player + title row
>   | "yt-community"     // text + image community post card
>   | "text-post";       // text-first (LinkedIn/X) — asset shown as attached media chip
> 
> export interface PublicationFormat {
>   id: string;
>   label: string;

> === PublicationPreview props ===
> import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
> import {
>   Bookmark,
>   Heart,
>   ImageIcon,
>   MessageCircle,
>   MoreHorizontal,
>   Music2,
>   Play,
>   Send,
>   Sparkles,
>   ThumbsUp,
> } from "lucide-react";
> import { aspectCss, availability, type PublicationFormat } from "@/lib/format-specs";
> import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
> 
> // PublicationPreview — spaces-evolution Amendment 21 (FR-SPACES-076). Display-only: renders an asset inside a
> // FAITHFUL chrome for each publication format (aspect + safe-zones + platform UI), so the user SEES how the post
> // will look before saving a draft / publishing. Zero network beyond the already-resolved owner-scoped signed URL,
> // zero mcoCoins. HONESTY (FR-SPACES-078): it is an APPROXIMATE preview — the published pixel is the asset as-is.
> 
> interface PublicationPreviewProps {
>   format: PublicationFormat;
>   assetUrl: string | null;
>   kind: "image" | "video" | "audio" | string;
>   caption?: string;
>   /** Brand persona shown in the chrome. */
>   handle?: string;
>   name?: string;
> }
> 
> const PERSONA = { name: "Gabriel AI", handle: "gabriel.ai" };
> 
> /** The asset itself, cropped to fill its slot (object-cover = the real crop each platform applies). */
> function Media({ url, kind, className }: { url: string | null; kind: string; className?: string }) {
>   if (!url) {
>     return (
>       <div className={cn("flex items-center justify-center bg-muted/40", className)}>
>         <ImageIcon className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground/60" />
>       </div>
>     );
>   }
>   if (kind === "video") {
>     return <video src={url} className={cn("object-cover", className)} muted loop playsInline autoPlay />;
>   }
> ```

> 14	--     + REVOKE PUBLIC/anon/authenticated + GRANT service_role (mirrors 20260603220000_secure_ledger_rpcs.sql).

> 18	-- ── VIEW per-tenant (RLS herdada via security_invoker — DD-AG-002 / FR-AG-001) ──────────────────────────────────
> 19	CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.collective_efficiency_ledger
> 20	  WITH (security_invoker = on) AS
> 21	  SELECT pr.id                                                        AS run_id,
> 22	         'orchestrate-content'::text                                  AS collective_type,
> 23	         pr.user_id                                                   AS user_id,
> 24	         COALESCE(jsonb_array_length(pr.steps), 0)                    AS n_units,
> 25	         pr.mco_cost                                                  AS mco_projected,
> 26	         pr.mco_cost                                                  AS mco_actual,
> 27	         pr.status                                                    AS result_status,
> 28	         (pr.status IN ('done'))                                      AS succeeded,
> 29	         (EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (pr.completed_at - pr.started_at)) * 1000)::bigint AS wall_clock_ms,
> 30	         pr.started_at                                                AS started_at
> 31	  FROM public.pipeline_runs pr
> 32	  UNION ALL
> 33	  SELECT ac.id,
> 34	         'autopilot-cycle'::text,
> 35	         ac.user_id,
> 36	         ac.planned_units,
> 37	         ac.spend_mco,
> 38	         (ac.spend_mco - ac.refunded_mco),
> 39	         ac.status,
> 40	         (ac.status IN ('completed')),
> 41	         (EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (ac.completed_at - ac.started_at)) * 1000)::bigint,
> 42	         ac.started_at
> 43	  FROM public.autopilot_cycles ac;
> 44	
> 45	COMMENT ON VIEW public.collective_efficiency_ledger IS
> 46	  'Collective Efficiency Ledger (agentic-governance FR-AG-001). Per-tenant DERIVED view (security_invoker=on → '
> 47	  'inherits SELECT-own RLS of pipeline_runs/autopilot_cycles). One row per collective run: n_units, mco_projected, '
> 48	  'mco_actual (spend-refund for cycles), succeeded, wall_clock_ms. Anti-Goodhart: derived from financial truth, '
> 49	  'never written to. SOP docs/processes/collective-efficiency-ledger.md.';
> 50	
> 51	-- ── RPC agregado cross-tenant (service-role-only — DD-AG-006 / FR-AG-002 / FM-AG-007) ───────────────────────────
> 52	-- The Sovereign's scale decision needs the WHOLE collective, across tenants. SECURITY DEFINER runs as the function
> 53	-- owner (RLS-bypassing) so it sees all tenants; access is hard-revoked from anon/authenticated and granted ONLY to
> 54	-- service_role. retry_rate joins the agentic-governance 'collective_retry' overhead events (FR-AG-003).
> 55	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.collective_efficiency_rollup()
> 56	  RETURNS TABLE (
> 57	    collective_type    text,
> 58	    runs               bigint,
> 59	    avg_mco_actual     numeric,
> 60	    avg_wall_clock_ms  numeric,
> 61	    retry_rate         numeric
> 62	  )
> 63	  LANGUAGE sql
> 64	  STABLE
> 65	  SECURITY DEFINER
> 66	  SET search_path = ''
> 67	AS $$
> 68	  WITH retries AS (
> 69	    SELECT (metadata->>'run_id')::uuid AS run_id
> 70	    FROM public.infra_health_logs
> ```

I've got the full schema for the scheduled_posts table now—it includes all the necessary fields for tracking social media posts across platforms, from scheduling through publication with error handling and retry logic. Now I'm ready to write out all ten documents, starting with creating the directory structure and authoring each file with the real code references I've identified. Defining the database schema for crew agents, pipeline runs, autopilot cycles, and scheduled posts, plus a view for tracking collective efficiency metrics across runs. I'm also planning to reuse existing format and platform specifications from the codebase.

I'm integrating the publication preview component with asset publishing through the video repurposing hook, setting up the agent task dispatch edge function to handle execution requests with cost deduction, and persisting canvas projects to a mirror table that tracks templates, execution counts, and publication status.

For the workflows table, I'm adding the core fields from the vision along with metadata like description, viewport, and timestamps to match the canvas projects structure. I'm reconsidering the delete restriction — since workflows are user configuration rather than financial records, deletion should be permitted for a user's own workflows.

Now I'm mapping out the functional requirements by part, starting with the Vista Operacional features: an "Operação" tab in the Orchestration page, a per-agent activity roster showing who's working or idle, activity descriptions reusing the existing message format, and a pending queue view.

I'm also defining status color semantics (green for active, gray for idle, amber for pending, red for failed), auto-refresh polling with tab-visibility detection, and filtering the operational view by selected workflow.

For Part C, I'm setting up the workflows table with RLS to ensure users only see their own workflows, and I'm starting to define the remaining functional requirements for multi-orchestration support. Finishing the Publish node with output formatting and preview reuse, then removing the Scene node entirely from the system. I need to define config inspectors for the three refocused nodes and clarify the run/dispatch semantics for each. Now I'm ready to start writing the actual specification files, beginning with the index and working through each feature requirement systematically.

  "content": "# MRD \u2014 Market Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** orchestration-control-room\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-07-22\n**BoK Applied:** BABOK v3 (Strategy Analysis \u00b7 Elicitation \u00b7 Requirements Life Cycle Mgmt)\n**Status:** Complete\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nO MCORCH j\u00e1 opera um **coletivo de agentes** (Pantheon/Constela\u00e7\u00e3o), um **motor de cria\u00e7\u00e3o** (Spaces) e um\n**pipeline de campanha** (`orchestrate-content`). O que falta \u00e9 a **camada de comando operacional**: uma\nsuperf\u00edcie onde o Sovereign *v\u00ea o coletivo trabalhando* e *configura m\u00faltiplos fluxos* para rodarem em\nparalelo. Hoje `/dashboard/orchestration` \u00e9 apenas um canvas de n\u00f3s solto, com 1 grafo em localStorage e\nn\u00f3s criativos (Cena) que duplicam o Spaces \u2014 n\u00e3o responde \u00e0 pergunta operacional central. Este m\u00f3dulo\nrefoca a superf\u00edcie em **Sala de Controle**.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Market Problem Statement (current state)\n\nO Sovereign expressou a dor diretamente (`.claude/context/orchestration-refocus-vision-2026-07-22.md`):\n\n> \"Meu objetivo \u00e9 visualizar **quem est\u00e1 trabalhando, quem est\u00e1 parado, o que est\u00e3o fazendo, o que est\u00e1\n> pendente, o impacto e o objetivo de cada workflow**. E vamos precisar de configura\u00e7\u00e3o de\n> projetos/workflows para ter v\u00e1rias 'Orquestras'/fluxos funcionando.\"\n\n**Estado atual (gaps materiais):**\n\n1. **Cegueira operacional.** Os dados existem espalhados (`agent_executions`, `crew_agents`, `pipeline_runs`,\n   `autopilot_cycles`, `scheduled_posts`, `collective_efficiency_ledger`), mas nenhuma tela os agrega numa\n   resposta \u00fanica a \"quem trabalha / o que est\u00e1 pendente / qual o impacto\".\n2. **Um \u00fanico grafo.** `/dashboard/orchestration` mant\u00e9m 1 grafo em `localStorage`\n   (`orchestration_positions_v1`), sem no\u00e7\u00e3o de \"projeto/workflow\" \u2014 imposs\u00edvel ter N Orquestras.\n3. **N\u00f3s desalinhados.** O canvas tem um N\u00f3 **Cena** (criativo) que pertence ao Spaces, e n\u00f3s de publica\u00e7\u00e3o\n   sem preview real \u2014 mistura os pap\u00e9is das tr\u00eas superf\u00edcies (Spaces=cria\u00e7\u00e3o, Constela\u00e7\u00e3o=roster,\n   Orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o=controle).\n\nIsto contradiz o Princ\u00edpio de Design n\u00ba 7 da doutrina ag\u00eantica (`agentic-vision.md` \u00a75): *\"Humano no comando\nem velocidade supra-humana \u2014 quando o coletivo produz mais artefatos do que o Sovereign consome, exigir\ndigest em camadas + bounded autonomy por dom\u00ednio.\"* Sem a Vista Operacional, o coletivo cresce **no escuro**.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Target Market Segments\n\n| Segmento | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o | Necessidade |\n|----------|-----------|-------------|\n| **Sovereign / Operador Zero (prim\u00e1rio)** | Gabriel, operando o MCORCH como Usu\u00e1rio 00 | Ver o coletivo em tempo (quase) real; rodar N workflows monetizados em paralelo sem perder controle |\n| **Tenant operador (secund\u00e1rio, futuro)** | Cliente multi-tenant que roda seus pr\u00f3prios workflows | Mesma vista, escopada por `auth.uid()` \u2014 a Vista Operacional j\u00e1 \u00e9 RLS-own por constru\u00e7\u00e3o |\n\n**TAM/SAM/SOM (estimativa qualitativa):** enabler interno \u2014 n\u00e3o \u00e9 uma linha de receita direta. Seu valor \u00e9\n**alavancagem**: destrava a opera\u00e7\u00e3o paralela de m\u00faltiplos funis (audi\u00eancia/receita), que \u00e9 onde a receita\nreal acontece. SOM imediato = 1 (Usu\u00e1rio Zero); SAM = todo tenant que atinge >1 workflow ativo.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Competitive Landscape (min. 3 alternativas)\n\n| Alternativa | O que faz | Por que n\u00e3o basta |\n|-------------|-----------|-------------------|\n| **n8n / Zapier / Make** | Editor de workflow visual + execu\u00e7\u00e3o | N\u00e3o conhecem o coletivo de agentes do MCORCH, nem o ledger financeiro (mco/resultado), nem o roster da Constela\u00e7\u00e3o; gen\u00e9ricos, n\u00e3o instrumentam \"quem est\u00e1 parado\" |\n| **Airflow / Temporal (DAG ops dashboards)** | Orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o de tarefas + observabilidade de runs | Engenharia-c\u00eantrico; sem camada de neg\u00f3cio (objetivo/impacto por workflow), sem preview de publica\u00e7\u00e3o social |\n| **Retool / dashboard caseiro** | Painel sob medida sobre as tabelas | Reconstruiria o que j\u00e1 temos em hooks; sem integra\u00e7\u00e3o nativa ao canvas de n\u00f3s nem ao dispatch `agent-task-execute` |\n| **Estado atual (`OrchestrationCanvas` solto)** | Canvas de n\u00f3s + Hist\u00f3rico | N\u00e3o responde \u00e0 pergunta operacional; 1 grafo; n\u00f3s criativos duplicando Spaces |\n\n**Diferencial MCORCH:** a Vista Operacional \u00e9 derivada da **verdade financeira j\u00e1 existente** (o\n`collective_efficiency_ledger` \u00e9 anti-Goodhart) e do **roster real** (`crew_agents`) \u2014 n\u00e3o \u00e9 um dashboard\ngen\u00e9rico, \u00e9 a sala de controle do *pr\u00f3prio* coletivo, com o humano no comando (ORO).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Market Opportunity & Timing\n\nO `collective_efficiency_ledger` (agentic-governance, VIVO) j\u00e1 mede **mco/resultado** e **overhead de\ncoordena\u00e7\u00e3o** por coletivo. A Constela\u00e7\u00e3o v2 j\u00e1 d\u00e1 o **roster per-user**. O `agent-task-execute` j\u00e1 despacha\nagentes reais (drill studio 6/6). O `PublicationPreview`/`format-specs.ts` j\u00e1 renderiza mockups fi\u00e9is por\nrede. **Todas as pe\u00e7as de dados e de rail existem** \u2014 falta a superf\u00edcie que as comp\u00f5e em comando. O timing \u00e9\nagora: o Sovereign est\u00e1 escalando distribui\u00e7\u00e3o (funis EP01/EP02) e precisa ver/controlar m\u00faltiplos fluxos.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Key Market Requirements\n\n| ID | Requisito de mercado | Prioridade | Tra\u00e7a p/ BR |\n|----|----------------------|:----------:|-------------|\n| **MR-OCR-001** | O operador deve ver, numa \u00fanica superf\u00edcie, **quem do coletivo est\u00e1 ativo vs parado** e o que cada um faz | Critical | BR-OCR-001 |\n| **MR-OCR-002** | O operador deve ver **o que est\u00e1 pendente** (fila de n\u00f3s, posts agendados, ciclos pr\u00f3ximos) | Critical | BR-OCR-002 |\n| **MR-OCR-003** | O operador deve ver o **objetivo e o impacto** (mco gasto vs resultado) de cada workflow | High | BR-OCR-003 |\n| **MR-OCR-004** | O operador deve poder **configurar e alternar entre m\u00faltiplas Orquestras** (workflows), cada uma com seu grafo e estado | Critical | BR-OCR-004, BR-OCR-005 |\n| **MR-OCR-005** | O canvas de orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o deve conter **n\u00f3s de orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o** (Agente, Port\u00e3o, Publicar) \u2014 n\u00e3o n\u00f3s criativos | High | BR-OCR-006 |\n| **MR-OCR-006** | Publica\u00e7\u00e3o a partir do fluxo deve mostrar **preview fiel por rede** e ser **privado-primeiro** | High | BR-OCR-007 |\n| **MR-OCR-007** | A vista deve ser **multi-tenant-correta desde o dia 1** (cada tenant v\u00ea s\u00f3 o seu) | Critical | BR-OCR-008, BR-OCR-009 |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Success Metrics (market-level KPIs)\n\n| KPI | Alvo | Fonte |\n|-----|------|-------|\n| Time-to-answer \"quem est\u00e1 parado?\" | < 3 s desde abrir `/dashboard/orchestration` | Vista Operacional (Parte A) |\n| N\u00ba de workflows ativos gerenci\u00e1veis em paralelo | \u2265 3 sem perda de contexto | tabela `workflows` (Parte C) |\n| Redu\u00e7\u00e3o de \"cegueira\" (perguntas ao agente sobre estado do coletivo) | \u2192 0 (respondido pela tela) | qualitativo |\n| N\u00f3s criativos no canvas de orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o | 0 (Cena removida) | registry `OrchestrationCanvas` |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Regulatory & Compliance Context\n\n- **LGPD:** a Vista Operacional l\u00ea apenas dados operacionais **j\u00e1 per-tenant** (`user_id = auth.uid()`).\n  Nenhuma PII nova \u00e9 introduzida. O rollup cross-tenant (`collective_efficiency_rollup`) **n\u00e3o** \u00e9 exposto\n  nesta superf\u00edcie user-facing (\u00e9 service-role-only, j\u00e1 existente).\n- **FMEA-011 (cross-tenant leak):** a **\u00fanica tabela nova** (`workflows`) nasce com RLS default-deny +\n  `auth.uid() = user_id` e passa `/security-review` antes do commit (mandato CLAUDE.md).\n\n---\n\n## 9. Traceability Matrix (stub \u2014 MR \u2192 BR)\n\n| MR | BR |\n|----|----|\n| MR-OCR-001 | BR-OCR-001 |\n| MR-OCR-002 | BR-OCR-002 |\n| MR-OCR-003 | BR-OCR-003 |\n| MR-OCR-004 | BR-OCR-004, BR-OCR-005 |\n| MR-OCR-005 | BR-OCR-006 |\n| MR-OCR-006 | BR-OCR-007 |\n| MR-OCR-007 | BR-OCR-008, BR-OCR-009 |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

ROI Model\n\n| Item | Estimativa |\n|------|-----------|\n| **Custo de constru\u00e7\u00e3o** | ~3 fatias de engenharia; 1 migration (`workflows`); reuso de 4 hooks + 1 componente de preview + 1 edge fn de dispatch; **0 novas edge functions**, **0 novos secrets** |\n| **Custo recorrente** | ~0 (leitura sobre \u00edndices existentes; polling client-side j\u00e1 usado em `useAgentActivity`) |\n| **Receita direta** | 0 (enabler interno) |\n| **Alavancagem (receita indireta)** | Destrava operar **N funis monetizados em paralelo** com controle \u2014 o gargalo declarado \u00e9 distribui\u00e7\u00e3o+atribui\u00e7\u00e3o (`feedback_full_force_revenue_autonomy`); esta \u00e9 a sala onde o Sovereign os comanda |\n| **Payback** | Imediato na primeira sess\u00e3o de opera\u00e7\u00e3o paralela (elimina \"cegueira\" \u2192 menos re-trabalho de coordena\u00e7\u00e3o) |\n\n---\n\n## 6. mcoCoins Economics\n\n| Opera\u00e7\u00e3o | Custo | Justificativa |\n|----------|:-----:|---------------|\n| Abrir/ler a Vista Operacional (Parte A) | **0** | Leitura/agrega\u00e7\u00e3o sobre tabelas RLS-own; nenhuma IA |\n| CRUD de workflows (Parte C) | **0** | Escrita de config numa tabela do pr\u00f3prio tenant |\n| N\u00f3 Agente dispara `agent-task-execute` | **reusa** | Cobran\u00e7a on-dispatch j\u00e1 feita pelo `agent-task-execute` via `deduct_mco_coins` (n\u00e3o-Sovereign); **nenhuma cobran\u00e7a nova** |\n| N\u00f3 Publicar (preview) | **0** | `PublicationPreview` \u00e9 display-only, zero-rede al\u00e9m da URL assinada j\u00e1 resolvida |\n| N\u00f3 Publicar (publish real, gated) | **reusa** | `publish-space-asset` \u2192 `scheduled_posts` \u2192 `auto-publish` (rail existente) |\n\n> **Anti-pattern proibido:** introduzir um novo `deduct_mco_coins` client-side ou um pr\u00e9-d\u00e9bito de workflow.\n> A cobran\u00e7a **sempre** roteia pelos RPCs at\u00f4micos j\u00e1 existentes nos rails reusados.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Constraints & Assumptions\n\n**Constraints:**\n- Harness Engineering: toda l\u00f3gica em hooks; componentes display-only.\n- A **\u00fanica tabela nova** \u00e9 `workflows`. A Vista Operacional \u00e9 100% leitura.\n- Nenhuma edge function nova; reuso de `agent-task-execute`, `publish-space-asset`, `orchestrate-content`.\n- Manter a rota `/dashboard/orchestration` e o `OrchestrationCanvas` (@xyflow/react) \u2014 refocar, n\u00e3o reescrever.\n\n**Assumptions:**\n- As tabelas-fonte da Vista j\u00e1 t\u00eam RLS-own **verificado** (confirmado: `pipeline_runs`, `autopilot_cycles`,\n  `agent_executions`, `crew_agents`, `scheduled_posts` s\u00e3o SELECT-own; `collective_efficiency_ledger` \u00e9\n  `security_invoker=on`).\n- O `collective_efficiency_ledger` j\u00e1 n\u00e3o distingue por `workflow.id` (n\u00e3o h\u00e1 coluna `workflow_id` em\n  `pipeline_runs`) \u2192 o impacto por-workflow na Fatia 1 \u00e9 agregado **por tenant/coletivo**, refin\u00e1vel depois\n  (OTD-OCR-004).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Business Risks (Risk Ledger)\n\n| Risk ID | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o | Prob. | Impacto | Mitiga\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---------|-----------|:-----:|:-------:|-----------|\n| **RISK-OCR-01** | Vazamento cross-tenant via `workflows` (FMEA-011) | Baixa | Cr\u00edtico | RLS default-deny + `auth.uid()=user_id` + `/security-review` SAFE + smoke de isolamento (mirror `smoke-asset-owner-signing`) |\n| **RISK-OCR-02** | \"Impacto por workflow\" prometer mais do que os dados suportam (n\u00e3o h\u00e1 `workflow_id` nos runs) | M\u00e9dia | M\u00e9dio | Lei 1: rotular o impacto como **agregado por coletivo/tenant**; refinamento por-workflow \u00e9 OTD-OCR-004 (deferido, aditivo) |\n| **RISK-OCR-03** | N\u00f3 Agente introduzir cobran\u00e7a dupla ou fora do RPC at\u00f4mico | Baixa | Alto | Reusar `agent-task-execute` (j\u00e1 faz `deduct_mco_coins` at\u00f4mico + idempotency_key); proibido novo d\u00e9bito client-side (BR-OCR-010) |\n| **RISK-OCR-04** | N\u00f3 Publicar publicar p\u00fablico sem inten\u00e7\u00e3o | Baixa | Alto | Privado-primeiro: default rascunho; publish real gated por GO (OTD-OCR-006), espelha `publish-space-asset` draft-first |\n| **RISK-OCR-05** | Migra\u00e7\u00e3o de localStorage\u2192`workflows` perder o grafo atual do usu\u00e1rio | M\u00e9dia | Baixo | Migra\u00e7\u00e3o one-time do grafo default para o 1\u00ba workflow; localStorage vira cache de fallback (OTD-OCR-001) |\n| **RISK-OCR-06** | Polling da Vista sobrecarregar (muitos tenants) | Baixa | Baixo | `refetchInterval` + `staleTime` (padr\u00e3o j\u00e1 usado em `useAgentActivity`); pausa em tab oculta; realtime deferido (OTD-OCR-007) |\n| **RISK-OCR-07** | Regress\u00e3o ao remover o N\u00f3 Cena (grafos existentes referenciam `sceneNode`) | M\u00e9dia | Baixo | Filtro tolerante no load (ignorar `sceneNode` \u00f3rf\u00e3o); remover do registry/palette/union de tipos |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Acceptance Criteria (business-level)\n\n- **AC-BR-1:** Abrir `/dashboard/orchestration` \u2192 aba \"Opera\u00e7\u00e3o\" responde \"quem est\u00e1 ativo/parado + o que\n  fazem + pend\u00eancias\" em < 3 s, escopado ao tenant (BR-OCR-001/002/008).\n- **AC-BR-2:** \u00c9 poss\u00edvel criar 3 workflows, definir objetivo em cada, alternar entre eles e ver o grafo de\n  cada um persistir entre reloads (BR-OCR-004/005).\n- **AC-BR-3:** O canvas n\u00e3o oferece mais o N\u00f3 Cena; oferece Agente/Port\u00e3o/Publicar funcionais (BR-OCR-006).\n- **AC-BR-4:** O N\u00f3 Publicar mostra mockup fiel por rede e opera privado-primeiro (BR-OCR-007).\n- **AC-BR-5:** `/security-review` da migration `workflows` retorna SAFE/NO FINDINGS; smoke de isolamento\n  cross-tenant conta 0 vazamentos (BR-OCR-009).\n\n---\n\n## 10. Traceability Matrix (MR \u2192 BR \u2192 PR stub)\n\n| MR | BR | PR (ver 03-prd) |\n|----|----|-----------------|\n| MR-OCR-001 | BR-OCR-001 | PR-OCR-001, PR-OCR-002 |\n| MR-OCR-002 | BR-OCR-002 | PR-OCR-003 |\n| MR-OCR-003 | BR-OCR-003 | PR-OCR-004 |\n| MR-OCR-004 | BR-OCR-004, BR-OCR-005 | PR-OCR-005..008 |\n| MR-OCR-005 | BR-OCR-006 | PR-OCR-009, PR-OCR-010, PR-OCR-012 |\n| MR-OCR-006 | BR-OCR-007 | PR-OCR-011 |\n| MR-OCR-007 | BR-OCR-008, BR-OCR-009 | PR-OCR-001, PR-OCR-005 |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

  "content": "# PRD \u2014 Product Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** orchestration-control-room\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-07-22\n**BoK Applied:** ProdBOK (Feature Definition \u00b7 MoSCoW) + CXBOK (Journey \u00b7 Luxury UX)\n**Status:** Complete\n\n---\n\n## 1. Product Vision Statement\n\n> Uma **sala de controle** onde o Sovereign v\u00ea o coletivo trabalhando em tempo (quase) real e comanda\n> m\u00faltiplas Orquestras em paralelo \u2014 cada workflow com seu objetivo, seu grafo e seu impacto \u00e0 vista.\n\n---\n\n## 2. User Personas\n\n### Persona Prim\u00e1ria \u2014 \"O Maestro\" (Sovereign / Operador Zero)\n- **Nome/Papel:** Gabriel, Lead Architect operando como Usu\u00e1rio 00.\n- **Objetivos:** ver o estado do coletivo num relance; rodar N funis (audi\u00eancia/receita) em paralelo sem\n  perder o fio; despachar um agente/diretor espec\u00edfico para uma tarefa; publicar do fluxo com preview fiel.\n- **Frustra\u00e7\u00f5es:** hoje precisa perguntar ao agente \"o que est\u00e1 rodando?\"; o canvas tem 1 grafo e n\u00f3s\n  criativos que n\u00e3o s\u00e3o orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o; nenhuma no\u00e7\u00e3o de \"projeto/workflow\".\n- **Tech-savviness:** alt\u00edssima (arquiteto). Espera densidade de informa\u00e7\u00e3o, n\u00e3o hand-holding.\n- **Padr\u00e3o de uso MCORCH:** abre `/dashboard/orchestration` para **comandar**, n\u00e3o para criar (isso \u00e9 o\n  Spaces) nem para ver o organograma (isso \u00e9 a Constela\u00e7\u00e3o).\n\n### Persona Secund\u00e1ria \u2014 \"O Operador Tenant\" (futuro)\n- **Papel:** cliente multi-tenant rodando seus pr\u00f3prios workflows.\n- **Objetivos:** os mesmos, escopados ao seu tenant.\n- **Necessidade de produto:** a Vista Operacional j\u00e1 \u00e9 RLS-own \u2014 a persona \u00e9 atendida por constru\u00e7\u00e3o, sem\n  c\u00f3digo dedicado.\n\n---\n\n## 3. User Journey Maps\n\n### Jornada A (happy path) \u2014 \"Ver o coletivo\"\n```\nAbre /dashboard/orchestration \u2192 aba \"Opera\u00e7\u00e3o\" (default) \u2192 em < 3s v\u00ea:\n  \u00b7 roster de agentes: verde=ativo, cinza=idle, \u00e2mbar=pendente, vermelho=falha\n  \u00b7 o que cada ativo faz (\u00faltima atividade leg\u00edvel)\n  \u00b7 painel \"Pend\u00eancias\": posts na fila + ciclos pr\u00f3ximos + n\u00f3s enfileirados\n  \u00b7 por workflow: objetivo + impacto (mco/resultado)\n\u2192 decide onde intervir.\n```\n\n### Jornada B \u2014 \"Rodar uma nova Orquestra\"\n```\nClica no seletor de workflow (topo) \u2192 \"+ Nova Orquestra\" \u2192 nomeia + define objetivo\n\u2192 vai para a aba \"Pipeline\" (canvas) \u2192 arrasta n\u00f3s Agente/Port\u00e3o/Publicar \u2192 conecta\n\u2192 ativa (is_active) \u2192 volta a \"Opera\u00e7\u00e3o\" e a acompanha.\n```\n\n### Edge case 1 \u2014 \"Agente parado que deveria trabalhar\"\n```\nV\u00ea agente em cinza (idle) num workflow ativo \u2192 abre o N\u00f3 Agente \u2192 confirma o agente/diretor\n\u2192 dispara tarefa (agent-task-execute) \u2192 status vira \"processando\" na Vista.\n```\n\n### Edge case 2 \u2014 \"Grafo antigo com N\u00f3 Cena\"\n```\nAbre um workflow criado antes do refocus \u2192 o canvas ignora graciosamente o sceneNode \u00f3rf\u00e3o\n(n\u00e3o quebra) \u2192 o usu\u00e1rio substitui por n\u00f3s de orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Feature Inventory (MoSCoW)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR tra\u00e7a | MoSCoW | Esfor\u00e7o | mcoCoins |\n|----|---------|---------|:--------:|:------:|:-------:|:--------:|\n| **PR-OCR-001** | Aba Release Phasing (alinhado ao A \u2192 C \u2192 B do Sovereign)\n\n| Fatia | Escopo (Must primeiro) | Features |\n|-------|------------------------|----------|\n| **Fatia 1 \u2014 Parte A** (menor risco) | Vista Operacional 100% leitura | PR-OCR-001, 002, 003 (+004 se houver janela) |\n| **Fatia 2 \u2014 Parte C** | M\u00faltiplas Orquestras | PR-OCR-005, 006, 007, 008 |\n| **Fatia 3 \u2014 Parte B** | N\u00f3s refocados | PR-OCR-012 (remover Cena) \u2192 009, 010, 011 |\n\n**Regra:** a Fatia 1 entrega o objetivo prim\u00e1rio do Sovereign **sem tocar em nenhuma tabela nova** \u2014 \u00e9 o\nmaior valor pelo menor risco. A tabela `workflows` s\u00f3 entra na Fatia 2.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Luxury UX Specifications (MCORCH / MIV)\n\n- **Paleta (tokens MIV):** fundo void; `--accent` cyan para ativo; \u00e2mbar para pend\u00eancia; vermelho para\n  falha; cinza para idle; **gold s\u00f3 para valor/impacto** (regra `reference_miv_design_tokens`: nebula s\u00f3 na\n  mem\u00f3ria, gold s\u00f3 em valor). O cart\u00e3o de Impacto usa gold para o n\u00famero de mco/resultado.\n- **Sem\u00e2ntica de cor de status (can\u00f4nica, herda dos n\u00f3s):** verde/emerald=ativo\u00b7done \u00b7 cyan=processando \u00b7\n  \u00e2mbar=pendente/aguardando \u00b7 vermelho=erro/bloqueado \u00b7 cinza=idle. Espelha os `CONFIG`/`STATUS` j\u00e1\n  definidos em `AgentNode.tsx`/`QualityGateNode.tsx`/`PublishNode.tsx`.\n- **Densidade:** a Vista \u00e9 um cockpit \u2014 informa\u00e7\u00e3o densa, tipografia pequena (padr\u00e3o dos n\u00f3s:\n  `text-[10px]`/`text-xs`), sem espa\u00e7o morto. Persona = arquiteto.\n- **Micro-intera\u00e7\u00f5es:** pulse no status \"running\" (j\u00e1 existe nos n\u00f3s); transi\u00e7\u00f5es suaves no seletor de\n  workflow.\n- **Acessibilidade:** cor **nunca** \u00e9 o \u00fanico sinal \u2014 cada status carrega r\u00f3tulo textual (ex.: \"Processando\",\n  \"Bloqueado\"), WCAG 2.1 AA (contraste). Reusar a rubrica s\u00eanior (`ux-ui-senior-loop`) na verifica\u00e7\u00e3o.\n- **Vision QA obrigat\u00f3rio** (`feedback_vision_qa_always`): print de cada superf\u00edcie nova passa por\n  `scripts/qa/vision-qa.ts` (custo 0) antes de declarar pronto.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Acceptance Criteria (Gherkin)\n\n```gherkin\nFeature: Vista Operacional (Parte A)\n  Scenario: Ver quem est\u00e1 parado\n    Given estou autenticado como tenant com >=1 agente no roster\n    When abro /dashboard/orchestration na aba \"Opera\u00e7\u00e3o\"\n    Then vejo cada agente com um status (ativo/idle/pendente/erro) em < 3s\n    And vejo a \u00faltima atividade leg\u00edvel de cada agente ativo\n    And N\u00c3O vejo nenhum dado de outro tenant\n\nFeature: M\u00faltiplas Orquestras (Parte C)\n  Scenario: Criar e alternar workflows\n    Given estou na sala de controle\n    When crio \"Funil EP01\" com objetivo \"distribuir cortes\" e depois \"Funil Receita\"\n    Then posso alternar entre os dois pelo seletor\n    And o grafo de cada um persiste ap\u00f3s recarregar a p\u00e1gina\n\nFeature: N\u00f3s refocados (Parte B)\n  Scenario: Canvas sem N\u00f3 Cena\n    Given abro a paleta de n\u00f3s\n    Then N\u00c3O existe a op\u00e7\u00e3o \"Cena\"\n    And existem \"Agente\", \"Port\u00e3o de Qualidade\" e \"Publicar\"\n\n  Scenario: Publicar privado-primeiro\n    Given conectei um output ao N\u00f3 Publicar e escolhi Instagram\n    When abro o n\u00f3\n    Then vejo um mockup fiel do post no chrome do Instagram\n    And a a\u00e7\u00e3o default \u00e9 salvar rascunho (privado), n\u00e3o publicar p\u00fablico\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Categoria | Requisito |\n|----|-----------|-----------|\n| **NFR-OCR-001** | Custo | A superf\u00edcie n\u00e3o introduz cobran\u00e7a de mcoCoins; leitura e CRUD de workflows = 0-coin |\n| **NFR-OCR-002** | Seguran\u00e7a/Tenancy | Toda leitura/escrita \u00e9 RLS-own (`auth.uid()=user_id`); `workflows` default-deny |\n| **NFR-OCR-003** | Seguran\u00e7a | A migration `workflows` passa `/security-review` antes do commit (FMEA-011) |\n| **NFR-OCR-004** | Billing | Qualquer d\u00e9bito (N\u00f3 Agente/Publicar) roteia por RPC at\u00f4mico existente (`deduct_mco_coins`); nunca client-side, nunca novo write-path |\n| **NFR-OCR-005** | Compatibilidade | Migra\u00e7\u00e3o graciosa: grafos antigos (localStorage / com `sceneNode`) n\u00e3o quebram |\n| **NFR-OCR-006** | Performance | Time-to-first-paint da Vista < 3s; polling n\u00e3o bloqueia UI; pausa em tab oculta |\n| **NFR-OCR-007** | Seguran\u00e7a de rede | O rail de publish herda os guards existentes (`fetchPublicUrl` anti-SSRF em `publish-social`) \u2014 nenhum novo fetch de URL do caller sem guard |\n| **NFR-OCR-008** | Manutenibilidade | Harness Engineering: 100% da l\u00f3gica em hooks; componentes display-only; nenhum data-fetch em componente |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Interface Requirements\n\n### 6.1 Contrato UI (componente \u2192 hook \u2192 fonte)\n\n```\nOrchestrationPage\n \u251c\u2500 OperationTab (display)            \u2192 useOperationOverview  \u2192 crew_agents + agent_executions\n \u2502                                    \u2192 useOperationPending   \u2192 scheduled_posts + autopilot_cycles + store\n \u2502                                    \u2192 useCollectiveImpact   \u2192 collective_efficiency_ledger (+ workflows.goal)\n \u251c\u2500 WorkflowSelector (display)        \u2192 useWorkflows          \u2192 workflows (RLS-own)\n \u2514\u2500 OrchestrationCanvas (display)     \u2192 useOrchestrationGraph + useCrewData\n       \u251c\u2500 AgentNode inspector         \u2192 agent-task-execute (reuse)\n       \u251c\u2500 QualityGateNode inspector   \u2192 agent-task-execute \u00d7 N (reuse)\n       \u2514\u2500 PublishNode inspector       \u2192 PublicationPreview + format-specs (reuse) + usePublishSpaceAsset\n```\n\n### 6.2 Contrato externo reusado (n\u00e3o novo)\n\n- **`agent-task-execute`** (edge fn existente): request `{ agent_id: uuid (required), task_prompt?, capability_key?, override_provider?, override_model?, payload?, idempotency_key? }`; JWT do user; cobra via `deduct_mco_coins` (n\u00e3o-Sovereign); escreve `agent_executions`.\n- **`publish-space-asset`** (edge fn existente): resolve o asset server-side owner-scoped \u2192 enfileira `scheduled_posts`; draft-first.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Data Requirements (valida\u00e7\u00e3o)\n\n| Campo (`workflows`) | Tipo | Regra |\n|---------------------|------|-------|\n| `name` | text | NOT NULL, 1..120 chars |\n| `goal` | text | nullable, 0..500 chars |\n| `status` | text | CHECK \u2208 {`active`,`archived`}, default `active` |\n| `graph` | jsonb | default `{\"nodes\":[],\"edges\":[]}`; forma `{nodes:[], edges:[], viewport?}` |\n| `is_active` | boolean | default `false` |\n| `user_id` | uuid | NOT NULL FK `auth.users(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE; **sempre** `= auth.uid()` (RLS + trigger opcional) |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Test Strategy Matrix\n\n| FR | Unit | Integration | E2E |\n|----|:----:|:-----------:|:---:|\n| FR-OCR-002/003 | mapper status/mensagem (mirror `buildMessage`) | query cruzada crew\u00d7execs | Vista mostra idle vs ativo |\n| FR-OCR-004 | agregador de pend\u00eancias | join scheduled_posts+cycles | painel conta itens reais |\n| FR-OCR-006 | deriva\u00e7\u00e3o de impacto do ledger | VIEW retorna por coletivo | cart\u00e3o exibe mco actual |\n| FR-OCR-009 | \u2014 | RLS: cross-tenant SELECT = 0 (smoke) | `/security-review` SAFE |\n| FR-OCR-010/014 | reducers do hook | create/update/graph persist | grafo sobrevive reload |\n| FR-OCR-015 | union de tipos sem `scene` | load tolerante de grafo \u00f3rf\u00e3o | paleta sem \"Cena\" |\n| FR-OCR-016/017 | dispatch mapper | agent-task-execute cria exec | n\u00f3 running\u2192done; gate bloqueia |\n| FR-OCR-019/020 | format resolver | preview por rede | mockup fiel; draft-first |\n\n**Smoke de isolamento (Lei 1):** `scripts/qa/smoke-workflows-isolation.ts` (espelha\n`smoke-asset-owner-signing.ts`): S1 owner l\u00ea o pr\u00f3prio workflow (200); S2 cross-tenant SELECT bloqueado; S3\nanon 401; S4 enumera\u00e7\u00e3o = 0. Zero-custo, herm\u00e9tico.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Traceability Matrix (PR \u2192 FR)\n\n| PR | FR |\n|----|----|\n| PR-OCR-001 | FR-OCR-001, FR-OCR-007 |\n| PR-OCR-002 | FR-OCR-002, FR-OCR-003, FR-OCR-005 |\n| PR-OCR-003 | FR-OCR-004 |\n| PR-OCR-004 | FR-OCR-006, FR-OCR-008 |\n| PR-OCR-005 | FR-OCR-009, FR-OCR-010 |\n| PR-OCR-006 | FR-OCR-011, FR-OCR-013 |\n| PR-OCR-007 | FR-OCR-012 |\n| PR-OCR-008 | FR-OCR-014 |\n| PR-OCR-009 | FR-OCR-016 |\n| PR-OCR-010 | FR-OCR-017 |\n| PR-OCR-011 | FR-OCR-018, FR-OCR-019, FR-OCR-020 |\n| PR-OCR-012 | FR-OCR-015 |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

Component Catalog\n\n### 2.1 New Custom Hooks (`src/hooks/`)\n\n| Hook | File | Responsabilidade | TanStack keys |\n|------|------|------------------|---------------|\n| `useOperationOverview` | `useOperationOverview.ts` | Cruza `crew_agents` (roster) \u00d7 \u00faltimo `agent_executions` por `agent_id` \u2192 status vivo + atividade leg\u00edvel (reusa a l\u00f3gica `buildMessage`) | `['operation_overview', user.id]` |\n| `useOperationPending` | `useOperationPending.ts` | Agrega `scheduled_posts(status='queued')` + `autopilot_cycles(status='running')` + n\u00f3s `pending` do store | `['operation_pending', user.id]` |\n| `useCollectiveImpact` | `useCollectiveImpact.ts` | L\u00ea `collective_efficiency_ledger` por `collective_type`; junta `workflows.goal` quando houver workflow selecionado | `['collective_impact', user.id]` |\n| `useWorkflows` | `useWorkflows.ts` | CRUD da tabela `workflows` (list/create/update/archive/remove); mirror de `useCreateCanvasProject`/hooks do Spaces | `['workflows', user.id]` |\n\n### 2.2 Extended Hooks\n\n| Hook | File | Mudan\u00e7a |\n|------|------|---------|\n| `useOrchestrationGraph` | `src/hooks/useOrchestrationGraph.ts` | Passa a carregar/salvar `workflows.graph` do workflow corrente (debounced 600ms); `localStorage` vira cache de fallback (OTD-OCR-001). `runPipeline` inalterado (segue chamando `orchestrate-content`) |\n\n### 2.3 New Display Components (`src/components/orchestrator/`)\n\n| Componente | File | Props | Hook |\n|-----------|------|-------|------|\n| `OperationPanel` | `operation/OperationPanel.tsx` | `{ workflowId?: string }` | comp\u00f5e os 3 abaixo |\n| `AgentActivityRoster` | `operation/AgentActivityRoster.tsx` | `{ rows: OperationAgentRow[] }` | `useOperationOverview` |\n| `PendingPanel` | `operation/PendingPanel.tsx` | `{ pending: PendingBucket }` | `useOperationPending` |\n| `WorkflowImpactCard` | `operation/WorkflowImpactCard.tsx` | `{ impact: CollectiveImpactRow[]; goal?: string }` | `useCollectiveImpact` |\n| `WorkflowSelector` | `WorkflowSelector.tsx` | `{ workflows; currentId; onSelect; onCreate }` | `useWorkflows` |\n\n### 2.4 Zustand Store Slice (mudan\u00e7a)\n\n| Slice | File | Mudan\u00e7a |\n|-------|------|---------|\n| `useOrchestrationStore` | `src/store/useOrchestrationStore.ts` | Remover `'scene'` do union `OrchestrationNodeType`; adicionar `currentWorkflowId: string \\| null` + `setCurrentWorkflow`; `setGraph(nodes, edges)` para hidratar do `workflows.graph`. Manter a API atual de status/positions |\n\n### 2.5 New DB Objects\n\n| Objeto | Tipo | Prop\u00f3sito | Migration |\n|--------|------|-----------|-----------|\n| `workflows` | Table | M\u00faltiplas Orquestras por tenant (grafo + objetivo + estado) | `<ts>_orchestration_workflows.sql` |\n\n> **Zero edge functions novas. Zero VIEWs novas. Zero secrets novos.**\n\n---\n\n## 3. Data Flow Diagrams\n\n### Use Case 1 \u2014 Vista Operacional (leitura, 0-coin)\n```\nUser            Hook                         DB (RLS-own)\n \u2502               \u2502                            \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[abre \"Opera\u00e7\u00e3o\"]\u2500\u2500\u25b6                      \u2502\n \u2502       useOperationOverview \u2500\u2500[SELECT crew_agents]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 crew_agents\n \u2502                            \u2500\u2500[SELECT agent_executions ORDER started_at DESC LIMIT 40]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 agent_executions\n \u2502       useOperationPending  \u2500\u2500[SELECT scheduled_posts WHERE status='queued']\u2500\u2500\u25b6 scheduled_posts\n \u2502                            \u2500\u2500[SELECT autopilot_cycles WHERE status='running']\u2500\u2500\u25b6 autopilot_cycles\n \u2502       useCollectiveImpact  \u2500\u2500[SELECT * FROM collective_efficiency_ledger]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 VIEW (security_invoker)\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[render roster + pend\u00eancias + impacto]\u2500\u2500\u2502\n \u2502  (refetchInterval ~12s; pausa em tab oculta)\n```\nNenhuma escrita. Nenhum `deduct_mco_coins`. RLS garante escopo por tenant.\n\n### Use Case 2 \u2014 Criar/alternar Orquestra (Parte C)\n```\nUser            useWorkflows                 DB\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[+ Nova Orquestra { name, goal }]\u2500\u2500\u25b6      \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2500\u2500[INSERT workflows (user_id=auth.uid())]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 workflows  (RLS WITH CHECK)\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[lista invalidada \u2192 novo item]\u2500\u2500\u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[seleciona workflow]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 store.setCurrentWorkflow(id)\n \u2502  useOrchestrationGraph \u2500\u2500[SELECT graph FROM workflows WHERE id]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 store.setGraph(...)\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[canvas hidratado com o grafo do workflow]\n```\n\n### Use Case 3 \u2014 N\u00f3 Agente dispara execu\u00e7\u00e3o (Parte B, reuse)\n```\nUser        AgentNode inspector      agent-task-execute (edge, reuse)     DB\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[escolhe agente + Rodar]\u2500\u2500\u25b6                                            \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2500\u2500[invoke { agent_id, task_prompt, idempotency_key }]\u2500\u25b6\n \u2502                                    \u2500\u2500[auth.getUser (JWT)]               \u2502\n \u2502                                    \u2500\u2500[deduct_mco_coins (n\u00e3o-Sovereign)]\u2500\u25b6 profiles (at\u00f4mico)\n \u2502                                    \u2500\u2500[dispatch groq/BYOK]               \u2502\n \u2502                                    \u2500\u2500[INSERT agent_executions]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6 agent_executions\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[status do n\u00f3: running\u2192done/error]\u2500\u2500  (Vista Operacional reflete no pr\u00f3ximo poll)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contract Definitions\n\n> **Nenhuma API nova.** Esta se\u00e7\u00e3o documenta os contratos **reusados** (fonte da verdade = os edge functions\n> existentes) para que o front os invoque corretamente.\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/agent-task-execute` (existente \u2014 N\u00f3 Agente / Port\u00e3o)\n```typescript\ninterface AgentTaskExecuteRequest {\n  agent_id: string;            // required \u2014 crew_agents.id (uuid)\n  task_prompt?: string;        // required p/ execu\u00e7\u00e3o LLM (n\u00e3o-capability)\n  capability_key?: string;     // opcional \u2014 roteia p/ edge fn alvo (constellation-v2)\n  override_provider?: string;\n  override_model?: string;\n  payload?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  idempotency_key?: string;    // anti-double-charge\n}\n// Auth: Bearer <user JWT>. Cobran\u00e7a: deduct_mco_coins (n\u00e3o-Sovereign) \u2014 at\u00f4mica, server-side.\n// Escreve: agent_executions (status, capability_key, result_summary, cost_mcoin, \u2026).\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/publish-space-asset` (existente \u2014 N\u00f3 Publicar, publish real gated)\n```typescript\n// Resolve o asset SERVER-SIDE owner-scoped \u2192 enfileira scheduled_posts (auto-publish drena). Draft-first.\n```\n\n### Nenhum novo ponto de dedu\u00e7\u00e3o de mcoCoins\n> A leitura da Vista e o CRUD de `workflows` **n\u00e3o** deduzem coins. O \u00fanico d\u00e9bito no fluxo B \u00e9 o que o\n> `agent-task-execute` j\u00e1 faz internamente. **Proibido** um segundo d\u00e9bito no client (NFR-OCR-004).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design (CISSP CBK)\n\n### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)\n\n| Componente | Amea\u00e7a | Tipo | Severidade | Mitiga\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|-----------|--------|------|:----------:|-----------|\n| `workflows` | Leitura cross-tenant | Information Disclosure | **Cr\u00edtico** | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` nas 4 ops; default-deny; `/security-review` (FMEA-011) |\n| `workflows` | Forjar `user_id` no INSERT/UPDATE | Tampering | Alto | `WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id)`; (opcional) trigger BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE que for\u00e7a `user_id := auth.uid()` \u2014 mirror do hardening `vm_canvas_projects` id-trigger |\n| `workflows.graph` | Inje\u00e7\u00e3o via jsonb (XSS ao renderizar) | Tampering | M\u00e9dio | O grafo \u00e9 dado do pr\u00f3prio tenant; render dos n\u00f3s \u00e9 display-only (sem `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`); labels via textContent |\n| Vista Operacional | Ler execs/pend\u00eancias de outro tenant | Information Disclosure | **Cr\u00edtico** | Todas as fontes s\u00e3o SELECT-own; a VIEW \u00e9 `security_invoker=on` (herda RLS do caller) |\n| N\u00f3 Agente | Bypass de cobran\u00e7a / cobran\u00e7a dupla | Tampering | Alto | Cobran\u00e7a s\u00f3 via `deduct_mco_coins` dentro do `agent-task-execute`; `idempotency_key`; nada client-side |\n| N\u00f3 Publicar | Publicar p\u00fablico sem inten\u00e7\u00e3o / SSRF em m\u00eddia | Elevation / SSRF | Alto | Draft-first (privado); publish real reusa `publish-space-asset` (owner-scoped) que herda `fetchPublicUrl` anti-SSRF |\n| N\u00f3 Agente | Agente de produto invocar fn de sistema/billing | Elevation | Alto | Barreira j\u00e1 existente no `agent-task-execute` (allowlist FR-CV2-012 \u2014 product agents nunca invocam auth/billing) |\n\n### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions (`workflows`)\n\n```sql\nALTER TABLE public.workflows ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;  -- default deny\n\nCREATE POLICY \"workflows: select own\" ON public.workflows\n  FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"workflows: insert own\" ON public.workflows\n  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"workflows: update own\" ON public.workflows\n  FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"workflows: delete own\" ON public.workflows\n  FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);\n```\n\n> **Decis\u00e3o (OTD-OCR-002):** delete permitido (\u00e9 config do pr\u00f3prio tenant, n\u00e3o ledger financeiro) \u2014 **n\u00e3o**\n> se aplica RESTRICTIVE no-delete. O default de UX \u00e9 soft-delete (`status='archived'`); hard-delete exige\n> confirma\u00e7\u00e3o. Contraste consciente com `space_publish_variants`/tabelas financeiras que t\u00eam no-delete.\n\n### 5.3 JWT Validation Points\nNenhuma edge function nova \u2192 nenhum novo ponto de valida\u00e7\u00e3o JWT a implementar. Os rails reusados\n(`agent-task-execute`, `publish-space-asset`) j\u00e1 validam JWT (padr\u00e3o `auth.getUser`). O acesso a `workflows`\ne \u00e0s fontes de leitura \u00e9 via Supabase JS com o JWT da sess\u00e3o (RLS enforce).\n\n### 5.4 Secrets Inventory\n**Nenhum secret novo.** Os rails reusados usam secrets j\u00e1 provisionados (per-user BYOK / groq). A Vista e o\nCRUD de workflows n\u00e3o tocam secrets.\n\n### 5.5 Reuso do N\u00f3 Publicar \u00d7 `format-specs` (detalhe de design)\n\nO `PublishNode` inspector resolve, para cada rede escolhida, um `PublicationFormat` do cat\u00e1logo can\u00f4nico\n`src/lib/format-specs.ts` (SSOT derivado de `channel_profiles`) e passa a `PublicationPreview`:\n\n```typescript\n// N\u00f3 Publicar (inspector) \u2014 display-only, 0 rede al\u00e9m da URL j\u00e1 assinada\nimport { PUBLICATION_FORMATS, availability } from '@/lib/format-specs';\nimport { PublicationPreview } from '@/components/creative/PublicationPreview';\n\n// para cada rede selecionada:\n<PublicationPreview\n  format={selectedFormat}      // PublicationFormat (aspect + safeZone + chrome)\n  assetUrl={upstreamAssetUrl}  // output do n\u00f3 upstream (URL owner-scoped j\u00e1 resolvida)\n  kind={upstreamKind}          // 'image' | 'video'\n  caption={caption}\n/>\n```\n`availability(format)` decide se a rede \u00e9 `real` (publish real via rail) ou `preview` (\"Em breve\") \u2014 honesto\npor design (Lei 1: nunca prometer alcance que o rail n\u00e3o entrega).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infra | Uso | Impacto |\n|-------|-----|---------|\n| `infra_health_logs` | **Nenhum novo write-site.** A Vista l\u00ea o que os m\u00f3dulos medidos j\u00e1 emitem (`collective_retry`/`collective_hop`) via a VIEW/RPC do ledger | Reuso do chokepoint (Princ\u00edpio n\u00ba 4 da doutrina) |\n| `collective_efficiency_ledger` (VIEW) | Leitura per-tenant (security_invoker) para o cart\u00e3o de impacto | Zero write; anti-Goodhart |\n| ChromaDB / mega-brain-daemon | N\u00e3o usados | \u2014 |\n| Knowledge Mesh (`mcorch_nodes`) | 1 n\u00f3 `documentation_suite` no seal desta BoK (Phase 7) | Inje\u00e7\u00e3o \u00fanica no seal, n\u00e3o em runtime |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Stub (a \u00daNICA tabela nova)\n\n```sql\n-- Migration: <timestamp>_orchestration_workflows.sql\n-- orchestration-control-room Parte C (FR-OCR-009). Mirror de vm_canvas_projects / spaces.\n-- M\u00faltiplas \"Orquestras\" por tenant: grafo denormalizado (1 fetch hidrata o canvas) + objetivo + estado.\n-- RLS default-deny + auth.uid()=user_id. Additive. Passa /security-review antes do commit (FMEA-011).\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.workflows (\n  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  user_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  name        text NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(name) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),\n  goal        text CHECK (goal IS NULL OR char_length(goal) <= 500),\n  status      text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active','archived')),\n  graph       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{\"nodes\":[],\"edges\":[]}'::jsonb,\n  viewport    jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{\"x\":0,\"y\":0,\"zoom\":1}'::jsonb,\n  is_active   boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\n  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.workflows ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;   -- default deny\n-- (colar as 4 policies de \u00a75.2)\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS workflows_user_updated_idx\n  ON public.workflows (user_id, updated_at DESC);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS workflows_user_active_idx\n  ON public.workflows (user_id) WHERE is_active = true;\n\n-- updated_at touch trigger (padr\u00e3o do repo)\nCREATE TRIGGER workflows_set_updated_at\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.workflows\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at();  -- reusar helper existente\n\n-- (OPCIONAL, hardening) for\u00e7a user_id = auth.uid() no INSERT/UPDATE (mirror do id-trigger de vm_canvas_projects):\n-- CREATE FUNCTION public.workflows_force_owner() ... BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE.\n\nCOMMENT ON TABLE public.workflows IS\n  'orchestration-control-room (FR-OCR-009): m\u00faltiplas Orquestras por tenant. graph = {nodes,edges,viewport} '\n  'denormalizado (mirror vm_canvas_projects.graph). RLS SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE own. Sem embedding (config, '\n  'n\u00e3o conhecimento). Nenhuma coluna sens\u00edvel.';\n```\n\n> **Sem `embedding vector(768)`**: `workflows` \u00e9 **config operacional**, n\u00e3o um n\u00f3 de conhecimento \u2014 n\u00e3o\n> alimenta `embed-mcorch-node` (contraste consciente com tabelas de n\u00f3s da malha). Nenhum \u00edndice HNSW.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Node Type Registry Changes (Parte B)\n\n| Arquivo | Mudan\u00e7a |\n|---------|---------|\n| `src/store/useOrchestrationStore.ts` | `OrchestrationNodeType` = `'trigger' \\| 'agent' \\| 'quality_gate' \\| 'publish'` (remove `'scene'`); default graph deixa de semear n\u00f3s; graph vem de `workflows.graph` |\n| `src/components/orchestrator/OrchestrationCanvas.tsx` | `nodeTypes` remove `sceneNode`; load tolerante ignora n\u00f3s `sceneNode` \u00f3rf\u00e3os |\n| `src/components/orchestrator/NodePalette.tsx` | Remove o item `{ type: 'sceneNode', \u2026 'Cena' }` |\n| `src/components/orchestrator/nodes/SceneNode.tsx` | **Deletar** o componente |\n| `nodes/AgentNode.tsx` (+ inspector) | Dropdown `crew_agents`; dispatch `agent-task-execute` |\n| `nodes/QualityGateNode.tsx` (+ inspector) | Multi-select de especialistas; bloqueio de downstream em falha |\n| `nodes/PublishNode.tsx` (+ inspector) | Coleta outputs upstream + escolha de redes + `PublicationPreview` |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Pattern Conformance Declaration (pointer)\n\n> Este m\u00f3dulo toca **\u22653 padr\u00f5es ag\u00eanticos de forma n\u00e3o-trivial** (Orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o/Multi-Agent, Human-in-the-Loop,\n> Tool Use, Evaluation & Monitoring, Resource-Aware) \u2192 **promove a Declara\u00e7\u00e3o para um documento dedicado**:\n> **[`09-pattern-conformance.md`](./09-pattern-conformance.md)** (copia o Master Template de\n> `docs/bok/agentic-governance/09-pattern-conformance.md` e preenche uma linha por padr\u00e3o). Retention & Archival Policy\n\n| Dado | Reten\u00e7\u00e3o | Pol\u00edtica |\n|------|----------|----------|\n| `workflows` (active) | Indefinida (config do tenant) | Vive enquanto o tenant existir (CASCADE em delete do user \u2014 LGPD erase) |\n| `workflows` (archived) | Indefinida, oculto da UI | Soft-delete; recuper\u00e1vel; hard-delete via confirma\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| Leituras da Vista | Ef\u00eameras (cache de query client) | Nada persistido pela superf\u00edcie |\n\n**LGPD:** ao apagar o usu\u00e1rio (`auth.users`), `workflows` cai por `ON DELETE CASCADE`.
